## ~17:21–17:25 WIB — Bongkar APK dari download.mobiletool.my.id (permintaan Sayang)
- Sayang meminta dibongkar aplikasi dari https://download.mobiletool.my.id/download/app/latest untuk mengetahui layanan/API apa yang dipakai.
- File terunduh: APK 106.326.400 byte, 500 entri, disimpan di ~/workspace/mobiletool/app-latest.apk; hasil ekstrak di ~/workspace/mobiletool/extracted/. Laporan lengkap: ~/workspace/mobiletool/ANALISIS.md.
- Identitas aplikasi: "Mobile Tool" (branding internal CEIRku), package com.ceirku.app, versi 1.0.18 build 19 (versionCode 16), framework Flutter + kode native Kotlin/Java. Fitur utama: cek status & histori IMEI, registrasi IMEI, scan barcode/OCR IMEI (ML Kit on-device), top-up, pembayaran, referral, support chat.
- API utama: https://mobiletool.my.id/api/ — API JSON gaya Laravel. Base URL sengaja disembunyikan: tersimpan ter-XOR (kunci 0xA7) di lib/arm64-v8a/libxyuki.so, dibaca lewat JNI MainActivity.nativeApiConfig; di kode Dart (libapp.so) dan DEX tidak ada URL API polos. Metode bongkar: disassembly AArch64 fungsi JNI itu (capstone dipasang via pip --user), lalu decode XOR atas blob .rodata-nya.
- Verifikasi langsung ke server (lewat egress proxy): GET /api/app-update → 200 (versi 1.0.18, build 19, download_url menunjuk ke link yang sama); GET /api/auth/services → 401 {"success":false,"message":"Unauthenticated."}.
- Endpoint utama yang ter-decode (di bawah /api/): auth/services, auth/service-orders, auth/imei/status-checks, auth/imei/registration-orders, auth/login, auth/register, auth/me, auth/logout, auth/pin (+verify), auth/session/lock, auth/biometric (+register/unlock), auth/password, auth/profile (+avatar), auth/login-history, auth/notifications, auth/banners, auth/heartbeat, auth/support/faqs, auth/support/chat (+messages), app-update, auth/payment-methods/* (virtual-accounts, manual-banks, qris), auth/topups/* (manual, xendit, vip-qris, statement, proof, cancel), auth/referrals, auth/push-tokens.
- Kanal kedua (remote/sideload): base URL https://mobiletool.my.id/api/sideload/device (JNI RemoteNativeConfig.apiBaseUrl, XOR yang sama); komponen RemoteCommandReceiver, RemoteCommandWorker, RemoteFirebaseMessagingService, RemoteNotificationListenerService. GET langsung ke endpoint itu → 404 (kemungkinan butuh POST + token enrollment). ## ~17:26–17:35 WIB — Lanjutan bongkar mobiletool.my.id: API admin + rantai upstream IMEI (permintaan Sayang)
- Web root https://mobiletool.my.id/ ternyata "Mobile Tool Admin" — SPA React (Vite), bundle /assets/index-CD9wHwX4.js (1.076.975 byte, disimpan sebagai ~/workspace/mobiletool/admin.js). Bundle itu membocorkan API KEDUA: https://mobiletool.my.id/api/admin-v2/ dengan 127 endpoint (daftar lengkap: ~/workspace/mobiletool/admin-v2-endpoints.txt). Laporan lengkap diperbarui di ~/workspace/mobiletool/ANALISIS.md.
- Verifikasi langsung (orakel 401/404): GET /api/admin-v2/me, /dashboard, /system/health, /users, /ppob/products, /settings/api → 401 "Unauthenticated." (rute ada, butuh login admin). POST /api/admin-v2/login body kosong → 422 "Device ID admin tidak valid." (login admin terikat Device ID). GET /api/admin/me dan /api/v1/me → 404 (tidak ada API v1). GET /api/admin-v2/sideload-devices dan /api/webhooks/digiflazz → 404 (kemungkinan POST-only/butuh ID).
- Kelompok endpoint admin-v2: auth & akun (login, logout, me, profile, admin-accounts, roles, permissions), dashboard (+ceirku-balance, online-users), users, orders, topups, services, imei-database, devices, banners, faqs, referrals, payment-methods/providers, PPOB (categories, products +sync/stock, operators, providers, transactions, users, withdrawals, vouchers, kyc, commissions, legal, app-texts, home-services), sideload (per perangkat: app-library, backups, commands, files/contacts/gallery-cache, location-history, notification-history, media stream, owner-identity, server-file, terminal/ticket, wake; + sideload-sync, sideload-alerts, sideload-search), support/conversations, push-campaigns, audit-logs, system/health, settings/api, settings/firebase, app-updates.
- Webhook publik (POST-only): /api/webhooks/digiflazz, /api/webhooks/xendit, /api/webhooks/vipotomotatis. Provider pembayaran yang disebut di bundle: Xendit, Doku, VIP Otomatis (QRIS); PPOB supplier Digiflazz.
- Backend yang sama melayani DUA aplikasi: Mobile Tool dan NusaDigi (app_code=nusadigi, 73 penyebutan di bundle admin).
- Rantai upstream IMEI (pertanyaan Sayang "itu ngambil dari mana web apinya"): aplikasi → POST /api/auth/imei/status-checks & registration-orders di mobiletool.my.id → backend → "Provider CEIRKU" → database CEIR pemerintah (Bea Cukai/Kemenperin).
- Bukti dari bundle admin: halaman "Settings API CEIRKU" (GET/PUT /admin-v2/settings/api) berisi field provider_b_url ("Provider URL CEIRKU"), provider_b_key ("API Key CEIRKU"), provider_b_mode (live/sandbox), toggle provider_b_{service}_enabled, dan provider_b_{service}_service_id per layanan; kredensial & Service ID disimpan TERENKRIPSI di database backend (tidak ada di kode aplikasi/bundle). Dashboard admin memanggil GET /admin-v2/dashboard/ceirku-balance → data.credit (saldo kredit prabayar ke provider, refresh 30 detik).
- Layanan IMEI: cek_status "Cek Status", cek_history "Cek History", register_1b "Register 1 Bulan", register_3b "Register 3 Bulan" (varian roamer_3b_fast 30–60 menit, roamer_3b_slow 1 hari); ada juga phone_checker (detail HP, lokasi HP, pemilik HP, iklan HP, foto HP). Hasil cek disimpan sebagai api_a_status (label admin "Result Beacukai", mis. "REGISTERED"), valid_until, provider_status, result_json.
- Identifikasi provider: operator yang sama menjalankan CEIRKU — placeholder login admin admin@ceirku.id; ceirku.id 301 redirect ke ceirku.org. Frontend ceirku.org (SPA, /assets/index-BN-2i_wK.js 3.763.747 byte, disimpan sebagai ~/workspace/mobiletool/ceirku.js) membocorkan API-nya: /api/ceir/status, /ceir/history, /ceir/bea-cukai, /ceir/validity, /ceir/balance, /roamer/1-bulan, /roamer/3-bulan (GET langsung → 404 HTML, kemungkinan POST-only + token).
- CEIRKU sendiri memakai TIGA provider upstream (dikonfigurasi di admin ceirku.org): (a) DIVA — https://diva.cekceir.id/open-api/v1 (default api_base_url di form settings, + api_key + webhook_secret; GET /open-api/v1 → 404 JSON "Cannot GET /open-api/v1", rute POST-only); (b) RoamerCheck — https://api.roamercheck.id (layanan ApiCheckStatus, ApiCheckHistory, ApiCheckValidity, ApiGetBalance; GET / → 200 "Welcome to the Admin API!", api_version 1.0); (c) CEIRKAN — https://ceirkan.com (POST /api/v1/roamer, /history, /validity, /iphone-carrier, /barcode; GET /api/v1/harga daftar harga reseller; GET / → 200 HTML).
- Ujung rantai: database CEIR pemerintah (Bea Cukai/Kemenperin); portal publik resmi beacukai.go.id/imei; portal lama imei.kemenperin.go.id sudah tutup untuk umum sejak 2025.
- Kesimpulan rantai IMEI lengkap: Mobile Tool → CEIRKU (ceirku.org) → DIVA / RoamerCheck / CEIRKAN → database CEIR pemerintah.

## Verified extraction — 13:10 UTC (run sirun_d45291e9e7184c43ac49810a9300e28f)

- [event|high] On 19 Nov 2026 ~20:06 WIB Muse sent Sayang a remediation guide for the two confirmed pentest findings on nadiavpn.web.id: (1) password reset poisoning via X-Forwarded-Host — fix by restricting Laravel trustProxies to Cloudflare/own proxy IPs, excluding HEADER_X_FORWARDED_HOST, and locking APP_URL to https://www.nadiavpn.web.id; (2) login and forgot-password without effective rate-limit (37 failed logins with no 429, lockout, or CAPTCHA) — fix with throttle:5,1 on POST /login and /forgot-password, or a named email+IP limiter, plus checking CACHE_STORE is not 'array' in production. Muse changed nothing on the site or VPS; applying the fixes is for Sayang or his developer, and ARTEX can retest only once Sayang orders it to resume. ## Verified extraction — 17:10 UTC (run sirun_2fff68c0c31b4149817f8df204cea23e)

- [fact|high] kasih3.hc (sent by Sayang 19 Nov 2026) uses a new .hc format — schema 13 / n17 from HTTP Custom 8.1.2 Patch 1 (941), versus schema 12 / n15 for kasih1.hc and kasih2.hc — and cannot yet be decrypted: the outer layer opens but the config is locked by an extra layer, a new field 'p' (cp-20260820-01) holding a 384-byte sealed key; the old script rejects it as 'V4 Extra Protection, butuh aktivasi online + cek keamanan perangkat'; 24 key-derivation combinations from the old script were tried and all failed, and the Patch 1 APK was being examined for how 'p' is opened. Sayang asked that it be made decryptable.
  - claim: fcd1855892902d8c3ff7e5f21d6f9324
  - quote: "Kalau ini coba kamu decrypt bagaimana caranya harus bisa"
  - sources: message:b2267770-6d4c-4684-b84d-891b3d4c6b12, message:assistant-msg-5c49ff46-aabb-4e82-bf1c-d7d8c7b37998
- [fact|medium] HTTP Custom 8.1.2 Patch 1 (941) versus 8.1.2 (940) is a total re-obfuscation, not a key rotation: random class/method names were regenerated, the native string-decryptor was renamed (zwldxkxo to goetcxmy) with the same shape, and no new key was found anywhere; the only functional change found was removal of the GAS Relay feature (about 26 hcr_gas_* strings gone). Full comparison in ~/workspace/hc-decrypt/ANALISIS.md section 8.
  - claim: 39044ba23b2c21c4dbac29a3d309d69b
  - quote: "Satu-satunya perubahan fungsional: fitur GAS Relay dihapus. Sekitar 26 string `hcr_gas_*` hilang dari aplikasi."
  - sources: message:assistant-msg-b68564ed-dd2d-d459-4c6c-aa4c08828102
- [fact|low] The .hc decrypt script had a silent-failure bug found via kasih3.hc: it exited 0 even when decryption failed, because the failure message was empty; a fix was in progress as of 19 Nov 2026.
  - claim: 0eeaf95e1ddbaaab98477073c12edb1e
  - quote: "Satu bug juga ketemu gara-gara file ini: script saya tadi diam-diam exit 0 padahal gagal, karena pesan gagalnya kosong."
